# Gap dossier: Integrated Pathway A+B trial design (clone surveillance)
**Status:** DEFERRED WITH EVIDENCE (2026-08-20)  
**Verdict:** Blueprint exists via **clonal hematopoiesis (CHIP/CH) trial frameworks**. Longevity stacks should gate repair/reprogramming behind CH risk stratification.

## Evidence
| Source | Finding |
|--------|---------|
| Mayo/blueprint 2025 (Br J Haematol lineage) | Guidance for early-phase CH therapeutic trials; avoid overmedicalization; public-health screening implications |
| Haematologica 2026 clinical decisions review | Dual framework: myeloid neoplasm risk + CV risk; CHRS stratification; CBC q3–6 mo high-risk; statin/ASA CV mitigation |
| Haematologica emerging therapies | No approved CH-directed drugs; IL-1β, vit C/TET2 (EVITA), IDH inhibitors in early trials; gene editing longer horizon |

## Proposed A+B trial skeleton (conceptual)
1. **Screen:** NGS for CHIP drivers + VAF + CHRS; exclude/defer high-risk TP53/splicing clones from aggressive repair arms.
2. **Arm sequencing:** CV risk optimization → anti-inflammatory/CH-directed (if trial) → pulsed senolytic/mTOR → optional tissue OSK.
3. **Endpoints:** dual — (i) aging surrogate/disease endpoint, (ii) clone VAF trajectory / incident MN / MACE.
4. **Stop rules:** accelerating VAF or new high-risk drivers → freeze Pathway-A intensification.

## Design implications (ultra-design L0/L5)
De-risk-first is not optional rhetoric — CHIP clinics already operationalize the surveillance half of Pathway B.

## Update (Ralph wake #211)
- **STL-101 human longevity NCT still absent:** **Wake #211 negative recheck** — **no human supplementation trial or protocol milligrams**; **bioRxiv 713446 (Mar 2026)** reports **oral queuine extends mouse lifespan 15.3%** and claims circulating queuine decline — **≠ human NCT**; **PLOS ONE 2021** in-vitro **µM** ladder only. **Sapience lucicebtide (ST101) ≠ Stellate STL-101 queuine** (**NCT04478279** GBM oncology, **Ozmosi Feb 2026** refresh).

- **ST101 ≠ STL-101 fence held:** **Wake #372** — Sapience **lucicebtide (ST101)** **NCT04478279** oncology GBM **ACTIVE_NOT_RECRUITING** (ASCO 2026 update) ≠ Stellate **STL-101** queuine preclinical. **Do not conflate** oncology IV peptide mg with queuine longevity milligrams.
- **Queuine plasma age-decline still UNSETTLED:** **Wake #372** — PLOS One 2021 shows **no significant age decline** in healthy adults (higher in women) vs **bioRxiv 2026** claim that circulating queuine declines with age; **no supplementation longevity NCT milligrams** as of **2026-08-23**.
